================ VLAN INSERTION TEST (TX context descriptor, TDES2.VTIR, ETH_MACVIR p.3126) ================
  frame sent: 60 bytes, EtherType 0x88B5 at bytes 12..13. With a tag the MAC 81 00puts  + tag there and
  shifts the rest by 4 bytes. RDES3.LT: 1 = type packet, 4 = type packet with VLAN tag; RDES0 = tag (RS0V = 1)
  ctx TDES3 = context descriptor after the DMA closed it: bit 31 OWN must be 0, bit 23 CDE must be 0
  id  len  bytes 12..17         LT  RDES0       ctx TDES3   | result
  A   60  88 B5 1E 1F 20 21   1   0x24004A40      -       | OK   no context, VTIR = none: frame unchanged
  B   64  81 00 00 64 88 B5   4   0x24005340  0x40000000  | OK   context tag A, VTIR = insert: MAC inserts tag A
  C   64  81 00 00 64 88 B5   4   0x24005C40      -       | OK   no new context, VTIR = insert: tag A still used
  D   60  88 B5 1E 1F 20 21   1   0x24006540      -       | OK   no new context, VTIR = none: no tag
  E   64  81 00 E0 C8 88 B5   4   0x24006E40  0x40000000  | OK   context tag B, VTIR = insert: MAC inserts tag B

================ VLAN INSERTION TEST (TX context descriptor, TDES2.VTIR, ETH_MACVIR/IVIR) ================

  shifts the rest by 4 bytes per tag. RDES3.LT: 1 = type, 4 = one VLAN tag, 5 = two VLAN tags
